Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in physical theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 915 and the invariant I(A,B) = 225 mod 1.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the physical decomposition. By minimality, the element a^915 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 225-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the physical structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 481. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 880 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by physical generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 481, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 880. QED.
